Function conv_full(a):
```
def conv_full(a):
    n=a.size
    L=1
    while L < 2*n-1: L <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, n=L)
    fa *= fa
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa, n=L)
    return c[:2*n-1]
```
But computing L each time overhead; precompute global? Top-level variables okay? We can define cache dict by n. Since length fixed, define in propose? No closures? We can have helper using module-level _CACHE = {}. But top-level.

Because evaluate exact uses np.convolve, our fast c may have tiny negative due to FFT; clip min 0.

Objective smooth:
```
def score_from_c(c, S, n): return 2*n*c.max()/ (S*S)
```
For fast objective, after clip c, if S small inf.

Gradient for weights w (length 2n-1) irrespective beta:
Given a, w (nonnegative sum arbitrary), grad_i = 2 * B_i where B_i = ∑_{j=0}^{n-1} a_j w_{i+j}. Use FFT:
```
def weighted_gradient(a, w, L, fft_W=None):
    # w length 2n-1. reverse w
    wr = w[::-1]
    fa = rfft(a,L); fw=rfft(wr,L)
    h=irfft(fa*fw, L)
    # B_i = h[2n-2-i]? from earlier coeff at 2n-2-i of conv(a, wr)
    # if h length L, take indices n-1 to 2n-2 and reverse? Let's derive: h_m conv coeff for m=0..2n-2. B_i = h_{2n-2 - i}. So B = h[2n-2 - np.arange(n)] = h[n-1:2n-1][::-1]? For i=0 -> index 2n-2; i=n-1 -> n-1. Yes h[2n-2: n-1:-1] => h[n-1:2n-1][::-1].
    B = h[n-1:2n-1][::-1]
    return 2*B
```
Check with active w only at k=n-1: w[n-1]=1. wr index 2n-2-(n-1)=n-1. conv a, wr: coeff m = h_m; B_i = h_{n-1-i? Wait 2n-2-i = for i=0:2n-2, but wr nonzero at m=n-1, coeff m=2n-2? conv index 2n-2 includes a_{?} wr_{2n-2-?}. h_{2n-2}=a_{n-1} wr_{?} maybe term: h_{2n-2}=∑_j a_j wr_{2n-2-j}. wr only at n-1, so j=n-1 -> a_{n-1}. For i=0, B_0 should ∑_j a_j w_{j} = a0*1? If active k=n-1, L_{k,i}=a_{k-i}=a_{n-1-i}. B_i should a_{n-1-i}. For i=0 -> a_{n-1}. h_{2n-2}=a_{n-1}; good. i=n-1 -> h_{n-1}; nonzero if j=0 wr_n-1 => a0; B_{n-1}=a0. Good. So gradient active center =2a_{n-1-i}, as expected. Great.

```
def mirror_step(a, grad, step):
    # a sum ~1, grad maybe mean? Use log update:
    # theta = step * grad
    # To prevent overflow, subtract max theta
    loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, tiny)) - np.max(np.log(a))
    new = np.exp(loga - step*grad) maybe if step grad positive reduce. But a may have zeros; parent positive? Parent maybe zeros? Need handle zeros. Better use score = a / max(a,tiny); new = a * exp(-eta*grad - max?). Multiply and normalize.
    r = np.exp(-eta*grad - np.max(eta*grad)) # to avoid overflow
    new = a * r
    s = new.sum()
    if s>0: new/=s
    else: new=a.copy()
```

Function `smooth_objective(p)`:
```
a = softmax(p)
c = conv(a)
maxc = c.max()
z = _CURRENT_BETA * (c - maxc)
# logsumexp z

b = weighted_corr(a, weights) # no beta
grad_a = 4*N*b  # because val derivative = 4N * sum weights ∂? Wait weights exp(z)/s = exp(beta c)/Z; d val/da = 2N*(1/beta)*∑ exp(beta c)*∂c/da? Let's derivative: smooth = ll/beta. d smooth/da = (β/β) ∑ w ∂c/da = ∑w 2B = 2B. So d val/da =2N*2B=4N B. Yes correct.
grad_p = a*(grad_a - np.dot(a, grad_a))
return val, grad_p
```

The log multiplier depends on a; not same as mirror `a exp(-eta grad_a)`. Which is better? Natural gradient reduces overly. Maybe use p step with `eta a*(grad_a - mean)`. If a small, no change. Could get stuck. We can add floor.

Mirror step:
```
def _mirror_candidate(a, B, scale=None, etas=(0.05,0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0)):
    grad = 4*n*B
    raw = grad - np.mean(grad)
    rmax = np.max(np.abs(raw))
    if rmax<=0: return []
    theta = raw / rmax  # in [-1,1]
    for eta in etas:
       shift = -eta*theta
       shift -= np.max(shift)
       new = a * np.exp(shift)
       s=new.sum()
       if s>0 and finite: new/=s
       yield new
```
But if a has zeros, new zeros. To allow, `a_pos = a + eps_floor/sum?` where eps_floor=1e-12/n. This changes score tiny. If source zero, exp cannot increase it unless we add floor. We can floor before mirror: `af = a + 1e-16*n?` Wait adding constant to all entries changes distribution; but by tiny amount, score tiny. If some entries need increase, floor gives a seed. Use `floor = 1e-12 / n` (total ~1e-12). negligible. `af = a + floor; af/=af.sum()`. Then mirror.

However, if best has exact zeros due to transfers, adding floor may slightly increase score but still valid. We'll not accept if score worse; current a used for score? Transfer move:
```
def _try_transfers(a, B, top=5, fracs=(0.01,0.05,0.15,0.3)):
    idx_order = np.argsort(B) # B raw proportional; high B should decrease, low increase
    sources = idx_order[-top:]
    sinks = idx_order[:top]
    for i in sources:
      for j in sinks:
        if i==j: continue
        # maybe limit q to 0.5*a[i]
        for frac in fracs:
           q = a[i]*frac
           if q <= 1e-18: continue
           b=a.copy(); b[i]-=q; b[j]+=q; b/=sum (same)
           sc=fast_score(b)
           if sc < best: accept break...
```

The full convolution coefficient in discrete includes both orientations. In continuous convolution of indicator functions, (I_A * I_B)(s)=∫ I_A(x)I_B(s-x) dx counts x in A, s-x in B. (I_B*I_A)(s) counts x in B, s-x in A. Since convolution is commutative, I_A*I_B = I_B*I_A as functions! Wait continuous convolution is commutative: (f*g)(s)=∫ f(x)g(s-x)dx = (g*f)(s) by substitution. It already counts both? Let's check discrete: np.convolve counts ordered pairs p as first; the coefficient for A positions and B positions includes both p in A q in B and p in B q in A? But continuous convolution formula ∫ I_A(x) I_B(s-x) dx: x is first component; if x in A, second in B. It does not include x in B, second in A. However by commutativity, ∫ I_A(x)I_B(s-x)dx = ∫ I_B(x)I_A(s-x)dx. Are these equal for each s? Yes because let y=s-x, ∫ I_B(y)I_A(s-y)dy. They are the same number. But in the sum over all x from 0 to1, the integral over x in A and x in B? Wait I_A and I_B are disjoint. The integral ∫ I_A(x)I_B(s-x) dx only has x where I_A=1; it cannot have x in B. How can it equal integral with x in B? Because the set of pairs (x,y) with x∈A,y∈B and x+y=s is in bijection with pairs (y,x) with y∈B,x∈A, but the integral with x∈B,y∈A counts the swapped pairs; they are different points in integration domain but have same measure. However the single integral ∫ I_A(x)I_B(s-x) dx counts only x∈A, not x∈B. The commutativity equality arises from variable substitution y=s-x, which maps x∈A,y∈B to y∈B,x∈A; the integral value equals the count of swapped pairs. But as a measure, the integral over x only one side; the equality says the number of A-B ordered pairs equals number of B-A ordered pairs, which is true. It does NOT mean sum is 2C; it equals C. In discrete full convolution, coefficient sums over all p. For p in A, q in B and p in B,q in A, total contributions =2 * (number of pairs with p in A,q in B) * AB. But continuous convolution of A and B indicators ∫ I_A(x) I_B(s-x) dx equals the number of p in A, q in B, i.e., C, not 2C. However in the expression H(s) = ∫ w(x)w(s-x)dx, w(x)w(s-x) product: if x in A, s-x in B, term AB; if x in B, s-x in A, term AB. The integral is sum over x of product; it includes x in A and x in B separately. We can write as ∫ I_A(x)I_B(s-x)AB + I_B(x)I_A(s-x)AB + ... The first integral C_AB, second C_BA. But C_AB=C_BA=C. So cross contribution =2AB*C. In the polynomial formula H = A^2(I_A*I_A)+AB(I_A*I_B)+AB(I_B*I_A)+B^2(I_B*I_B). Since I_A*I_B=I_B*I_A=C, cross=2AB C. But often if one expands w^2 convolution, w*w = (I_A A + I_B B)*(I_A A + I_B B) = A^2 I_A*I_A + AB(I_A*I_B + I_B*I_A)+B^2 I_B*I_B. Yes cross doubled. Continuous convolution commutativity gives I_A*I_B=I_B*I_A, not collapse to one. So our double factor correct.

Candidate generation:
- `K` from 2 to 10. For each K, generate length fractions using Dirichlet random. But integer lengths must sum n. We can take `w = rng.dirichlet(alpha)` with alpha maybe 1 or 2; lengths = max(1, floor(w*(n-K))) then adjust.
- Heights: generate shapes:
   * lognormal random: h = exp(rng.normal(0, sigma)), normalize.
   * linear: h_i = 1 + i/(K-1)*amp + noise.
   * cosine: h_i = 1 + amp*cos(2π f i/(K-1)+phase), clip to min.
   * U/O shapes: h_i = 1 + shape_power*((i/(K-1)-0.5)^2)
   * random two-level: base 1, some blocks amp high/low.
   * step patterns: monotone up/down.
We can generate many (e.g., 200) and evaluate fast score. Since each eval FFT ~0.01, 200=2s. Good. Select best top e.g., 12 by score.
- Also include initial parent maybe.

Block coordinate optimization:
For each selected profile, run height optimization for a limited time. Since profiles low dims, can do more. We'll write function that returns improved `(lengths, heights, score)`.
Pseudo:
```
def _optimize_heights(lengths, heights, max_sweeps, rng):
    a = np.repeat(heights, lengths)
    slices = [(i,i+l) ...]
    best_score=fast_score(a)
    for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
       order=random shuffle if rng else range
       for b in order:
           sl=slices[b]
           cur=heights[b]
           # log range around cur
           lc=np.log(max(cur,1e-12))
           lo=lc-3; hi=lc+3
           # grid 31
           logs=np.linspace(lo,hi,31)
           vals=[]
           for lg in logs:
              x=max(math.exp(lg),0)
              a[sl]=x
              vals.append(fast_score(a))
           a[sl]=cur
           if min(vals) < best_score: set best slice; update best_score; sweep improved.
           # if boundary, maybe expand not necessary with 6 range.
           # Use scipy minimize_scalar with bounds [exp(lo),exp(hi)] maybe if time, but grid enough.
       # after sweep, optionally normalize heights = heights/sum to avoid scale drift
       s=heights.sum(); if s>0: heights=heights/s
    return lengths, heights, best_score, a
```
But if current height is zero, log issue. We can allow zero? Coordinate can find if optimum zero. If cur=0, set lc=log(1e-9), range [-10,0]? But blocks with zero might be dropped; we could remove zero blocks by merging? Not necessary. Ensure min height tiny 1e-9.
Grid around current may miss if optimum far (e.g., a block should be 10x current). We can use wider range initially: [-5,5] (factor 148) maybe too many grid? 41 points. For K small, okay. Better: Do a global scan per block ignoring current: log range [log(min_h), log(max_h)+2]. Heights can be from 1e-3 to 100 relative. Since score scale invariant, relative values matter; total sum not fixed. We can normalize heights sum1 after each block; but individual optimum relative can be up to maybe 10. Use global log bounds [log(1e-3), log(10)] = [-6.9,2.3]. For one block, grid 40. This can jump. But if other heights sum ~1, x could be outside? Better combine: candidate `x = cur * factor` where factors log spaced [1e-4,1e4], 30 points. This is global relative and finite. For cur=0, use 1e-5*factor.
`factors = np.exp(np.linspace(math.log(1e-5), math.log(1e5), 33))`? Too wide but okay 33. Each height eval FFT. K=6, sweep 10 ->1980 eval ~20s per candidate; many too much. Use 21 factors maybe.

```
orig = float(heights[b]); a[sl]=orig
for factor in factors:
  x = max(orig*factor, 0)
  a[sl]=x
  sc=fast_score(a)
  if sc < best_local: ...
a[sl]=orig
```
Fast_score allocates FFT arrays; in-place a okay. No need copy.

Block profile scoring: We can build vector via `np.repeat` but to set slices in coordinate, precompute start indices. For each candidate, generate `lengths` and `heights`; `a = np.repeat(heights, lengths)`. Check sum finite. Score.

Lengths generation details:
```
def random_lengths(K, rng):
  # ensure at least 1 each
  weights = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(K)*1.0)
  lengths = np.floor(weights*(n-K)).astype(int)+1
  while sum(lengths)<n: add random
  while sum(lengths)>n: decrement >1
```

Let's implement `_optimize_block_profile(lengths, heights, rng, max_sweeps, eval_budget)` more concretely:
```
def _optimize_block_profile(lengths, heights, rng, max_sweeps=8):
    n=sum(lengths)
    heights = np.asarray(heights,float); lengths=int
    # normalize heights to sum1 initially
    h=heights/heights.sum()
    a=np.repeat(h,lengths)
    starts = np.concatenate(([0], np.cumsum(lengths)[:-1]))
    ends = np.cumsum(lengths)
    best_score=fast_score(a)
    factors = np.exp(np.linspace(-4.0,4.0,25))
    for sw in range(max_sweeps):
       order = rng.permutation(K)
       improved=False
       for b in order:
          start=starts[b]; stop=ends[b]
          orig = h[b]
          base = orig if orig>1e-12 else np.min(h[h>1e-12]) if any else 1e-6
          # If base too small, use 1/n
          if base <=0: base=1.0/n
          local_best = best_score
          local_x = orig
          for f in factors:
             x = base * f
             if x <= 1e-15: x=1e-15
             a[start:stop] = x
             sc=fast_score(a)
             if sc < local_best - 1e-12:
                 local_best = sc; local_x=x
          if local_x != orig:
              h[b]=local_x
              a[start:stop]=local_x
              if local_best < best_score:
                  best_score=local_best; improved=True
              # if not lower global but local? local_best should be <= best? Maybe due changes can increase global? we only update if local_x not orig; even if score not lower than best? We might accept neutral to explore? Better only if local_best < best_score.
              # If local_best > orig score? factors include orig so local_best <= orig score which maybe best. So okay.
          else:
              a[start:stop]=orig
       if improved:
          # normalize heights and a
          s=h.sum(); h/=s; a=np.repeat(h,lengths); best_score=fast_score(a) # should same
       else:
          # maybe try wider factors or break after few no improve
          # We can attempt a wider random factor list for each block or break
          # To save, break
          break
    # final exact? continue
    return h,lengths,best_score,a
```

Mirror phase implementation details:
```
def _run_mirror_phase(budget_s):
   a = _G_BEST_A.copy()
   current = _fast_score(a)
   if current != _G_BEST_SCORE: maybe update?
   a = np.maximum(a, FLOOR); a/=a.sum(); current = _fast_score(a)
   update best if lower
   r = _RNG
   modes = ['exact','soft0.01','soft0.02','soft0.05','soft0.1','soft0.25']
   cycle=0
   no_improve=0
   while time < _DEADLINE - 30:
      c = _conv_self(a)
      M = float(c.max())
      mode = modes[cycle % len(modes)]
      if mode.startswith('exact'):
         tol = max(1e-12, M*1e-7)
         mask = c >= M - tol
         if not mask.any(): mask[c.argmax()]=True
         w = mask.astype(float); w/=w.sum()
      else:
         temp = float(mode[4])*M
         z = (c-M)/max(temp,1e-300)
         e=np.exp(z-min(z)); s=e.sum(); w=e/s
      fa = np.fft.rfft(a, _G_L)
      B = _corr_from_fa(fa,w); B=np.maximum(B,0)
      grad = 4.0*n*B
      raw = grad - grad.mean()
      rmax = np.max(np.abs(raw))
      improved=False
      if rmax > 1e-14:
         theta = raw / rmax
         for eta in (0.03,0.08,0.2,0.5): # log change <=0.5
            shift = -eta * theta
            shift -= shift.max()
            new = a * np.exp(shift)
            snew = new.sum()
            if snew <=0 or not finite: continue
            new /= snew
            sc = _fast_score(new)
            if sc < current - 1e-12:
                a = new; current = sc; improved=True
                if current < _G_BEST_SCORE - 1e-12: update best
                break
      if not improved:
         # transfer
         # try top raw sources and sinks
         order = np.argsort(raw)
         sources=order[-3:]; sinks=order[:3]
         for i in sources:
           for j in sinks:
             if i==j: continue
             ai = a[i]
             for frac in (0.01,0.05,0.15):
                q=ai*frac
                if q<=0: continue
                new=a.copy(); new[i]=ai-q; new[j]=a[j]+q; s=new.sum(); new/=s
                sc=_fast_score(new)
                if sc < current - 1e-12: update; improved=True; break
             if improved: break
           if improved: break
      if not improved:
         no_improve +=1
         if no_improve > 30:
            # random perturbation of current (not best)
            sigma = 0.02 + 0.02*min(no_improve//10, 5)
            noisy = a * np.exp(np.random.normal(0,sigma,n))
            noisy = np.maximum(noisy, FLOOR); noisy/=noisy.sum()
            sc = _fast_score(noisy)
            if sc < current + 0.02: # allow slight worsen to escape
                a = noisy; current = sc
            no_improve=0
         else:
            a = a # keep
      cycle +=1
   return best
```
Need avoid too many transfer evals: 3x3x3=27 worst. Okay. Use `np.random` via `_RNG`? We have `_RNG.normal`. Use `_RNG.standard_normal(n)`.

But `raw` not normalized? rmax scaling; theta in [-1,1]. Eta=0.5 can multiply entries by e^0.5=1.65. Good. For exact active center in symmetric block, raw may be zero? If active constraints symmetric and a symmetric, no move. Soft weights break. If no improve, transfers can.

Should we center raw before mirror? We subtract mean. If raw has non-zero mean due weights, subtract mean to preserve first-order? Good.

Transfer: If raw all zero, no transfer. If raw sorted, high raw source, low sink. But if some a zero and raw high? source could have zero? If a_i=0 but gradient positive? possible? Then q=0 no. Sinks low may have zero; transfer increases. Good.

```
def _block_param_objective(x):
   lx = x[:K]; exp_l = exp(lx - max); lengths = floor(exp_l/sum * n) adjusted
   lh = x[K:]; h = exp(lh - max); if sum<=0: inf
   a = repeat(h,sum)
   return _fast_score(a)
```

```
def _lengths_from_log(lx, n, K):
   e=np.exp(lx - np.max(lx)); w=e/e.sum(); lengths=np.floor(w*n).astype(int); lengths=np.maximum(lengths,1); rem=n-lengths.sum(); if rem>0 add to random/largest until sum; if rem<0 decrement >1.
```

`_fft_len(n)`:
```
def _fft_len(n):
    L=1
    m=2*n-1
    while L<m: L <<=1
    return L
```

`_normalize(a)`:
```
def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
       return np.ones(a.size if a.size else 6962, dtype=np.float64)/ (a.size if a.size else 6962)
    a /= s
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s2 = a.sum()
    if s2>0: a/=s2
    return a
```
If size 0? not.

```
def _conv_self(a):
```

`_corr_from_fa(fa, weights)`:
```
def _corr_from_fa(fa, weights):
    n=_G_N; L=_G_L
    w_rev = weights[::-1]
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w_rev, n=L)
    b=np.fft.irfft(fa*fw, n=L)
    b = b[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
    if b.size and b.min()<0: b = np.maximum(b,0) # But creating new each time. Use np.clip.
    return b
```
`weights` may not be contiguous; `weights[::-1]` view. Good.

`_fast_score(a)`:
```
def _fast_score(a):
    n=_G_N
    S=float(np.sum(a))
    if S < 1e-9 or not finite: return np.inf
    c=_conv_self(a)
    m=float(c.max())
    if not finite(m): return np.inf
    return float(2.0*n*m/(S*S))
```
Could use max without abs; c clean.

`_softmax_p(p)`:
```
def _softmax_p(p):
    n=_G_N
    if p is None: return ones/n
    z = np.array(p, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    if not finite all: return ones/n
    z -= float(np.mean(z))
    m=float(np.max(z))
    if not finite(m): return ones/n
    z -= m
    z = np.maximum(z, -745.0)
    a=np.exp(z)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s<=0 or not finite(s): return ones/n
    a /= s
    # floor tiny
    floor = 1e-300? If use 1e-18/n, total 1e-14. Let's use 1e-25/n negligible.
    floor = 1e-25 / n
    if a.min() < floor:
       a = np.maximum(a, floor); a /= a.sum()
    return a
```

`_smooth_objective(p)`:
```
def _smooth_objective(p):
    global _G_BEST_SCORE, _G_BEST_A, _G_BEST_P
    n=_G_N; L=_G_L; beta=_G_CURRENT_BETA
    if beta <=0 or not finite(beta): beta=1000.0; _CURRENT_BETA=beta? no global not necessary.
    a=_softmax_p(p)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, n=L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, n=L)
    c=c[:2*n-1]
    if c.min()<0: c=np.maximum(c,0)
    maxc=float(c.max())
    if not finite(maxc) or maxc<0: maxc=0
    exact=2.0*n*maxc
    if finite(exact) and exact < _G_BEST_SCORE - 1e-12:
       _G_BEST_SCORE=exact; _G_BEST_A=a.copy(); _G_BEST_P=np.array(p, copy=True)
    if maxc == 0:
       return 2*n*1e-300, np.zeros_like(a) #?
    z = beta*(c - maxc)
    # z max 0
    # if beta*maxc large? use stable; if z min < -745 underflow zero okay. To avoid all underflow if only max exact, z has 0 at max, exp 1.
    expz=np.exp(np.maximum(z, -745.0))
    s=float(expz.sum())
    if s<=0 or not finite(s): s=1.0
    weights=expz/s
    log_s = math.log(s)
    smooth = maxc + log_s / beta
    val = 2.0*n*smooth
    B=_corr_from_fa(fa, weights)
    if B.min()<0: B=np.maximum(B,0)
    grad_a = 4.0*n*B
    dm = float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
    grad_p = a*(grad_a - dm)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_p)):
       grad_p = np.zeros_like(a)
       val = exact
    return float(val), grad_p
```
If beta huge, z min -745 some exp zero; weights count max; s maybe count. good.

Potential overflow in `beta*(c-maxc)`: beta maybe 1e9, c diff ~1e-4 ->1e5 no overflow. If beta >1e300? no. We choose beta ~ T/maxc <=300/1e-4=3e6. fine.

`_run_lbfgs(p0, beta, maxfun, maxiter)`:
```
def _run_lbfgs(p0, beta, maxfun=300, maxiter=100):
   global _G_CURRENT_BETA
   if scipy is None: return
   _G_CURRENT_BETA = beta
   p0 = np.asarray(p0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
   p0 -= p0.mean()
   try:
      res = scipy.optimize.minimize(_smooth_objective, p0, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', callback=_lbfgs_callback, options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'maxfun': maxfun, 'ftol': 1e-12, 'gtol': 1e-5, 'maxcor':10, 'maxls':20, 'disp':False})
   except Exception as e:
      print('L-BFGS error:', e)
```
Maybe `method='L-BFGS-B'` requires no bounds; okay. Could use `method='BFGS'` for unconstrained maybe slower; L-BFGS-B okay.

Implementation of `_optimize_block_profile` in final code (careful no lambda):
```
def _optimize_block_profile(lengths, heights, rng, max_sweeps):
    lengths = np.asarray(lengths, dtype=np.int64)
    heights = np.asarray(heights, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    s = float(heights.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
       heights = np.ones_like(heights)
    else:
       heights /= s
    a = np.repeat(heights, lengths)
    starts = np.concatenate(([0], np.cumsum(lengths)[:-1]))
    ends = np.cumsum(lengths)
    K = lengths.size
    factors = np.exp(np.linspace(-3.5, 3.5, 21))
    best_score = _fast_score(a)
    for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
       if time.monotonic() > _G_DEADLINE: break
       order = rng.permutation(K)
       improved = False
       for b in order:
           orig = float(heights[b])
           base = orig if orig > 1e-12 else float(np.min(heights[heights > 1e-12])) if np.any(heights > 1e-12) else 1.0/_G_N
           if base <= 1e-15: base = 1.0/_G_N
           start = int(starts[b]); stop=int(ends[b])
           local_best = _fast_score(a) # current? But a currently has best global? After previous block changes, a corresponds to current heights. Could use current block score. Simpler local_best = best_score? But if other changes not improving, best_score maybe from previous; current a score may be >best? We only update height if local_best improves best_score; so current a should be best? If a includes changes that were neutral? We set only if improved; so a should be best. Use local_best=best_score.
           local_x = orig
           for f in factors:
               x = base * float(f)
               if x < 1e-15: x = 1e-15
               a[start:stop] = x
               sc = _fast_score(a)
               if sc < local_best:
                   local_best = sc; local_x = x
           if local_x != orig:
               heights[b] = local_x
               a[start:stop] = local_x
               if local_best < best_score:
                   best_score = local_best; improved = True
           else:
               a[start:stop] = orig
       if not improved:
           # one wider attempt? Maybe break
           break
       # normalize heights and rebuild
       hsum = float(heights.sum())
       if hsum > 0: heights /= hsum
       a = np.repeat(heights, lengths)
       best_score = _fast_score(a)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    a = a / a.sum()
    # rescale heights to a max? Not needed
    return best_score, a, heights, lengths
```

`_random_lengths`:
```
def _random_lengths(K, rng, alpha):
    n=_G_N
    w = rng.dirichlet(alpha * np.ones(K))
    lengths = np.maximum(1, np.floor(w * n).astype(np.int64))
    diff = n - int(lengths.sum())
    while diff > 0:
        idx = int(rng.integers(K))
        lengths[idx] += 1
        diff -= 1
    while diff < 0:
        # decrement largest length >1
        idx = int(np.argmax(lengths))
        if lengths[idx] <= 1:
           # find a >1
           candidates = np.where(lengths > 1)[0]
           if candidates.size == 0: break
           idx = int(rng.choice(candidates))
        lengths[idx] -= 1
        diff += 1
    return lengths.tolist()
```
If K > n? K small. Good.

`_random_heights`:
```
def _random_heights(K, rng, mode):
    t = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,max(1,K))
    h = np.ones(K)
    amp = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 2.5))
    if mode ==0:
       sig = float(rng.choice([0.2,0.5,1.0]))
       h = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sig, K))
    elif mode==1:
       h = 1.0 + amp*(2.0*t -1.0)
    elif mode==2:
       h = 1.0 + amp*t
    elif mode==3:
       h = 1.0 + amp*(1.0-t)
    elif mode==4:
       h = 1.0 + amp*4.0*(t-0.5)**2
    elif mode==5:
       f = float(rng.uniform(1.0,4.0)); ph=float(rng.uniform(0,2*math.pi)); h=1+amp*np.cos(2*math.pi*f*t+ph)
    elif mode==6: sin
    else:
       h = 1.0 + amp*(rng.random(K)>0.5)
    h = np.clip(h, 1e-3, None)
    s=h.sum(); h/=s
    return h
```
`np.choice` returns numpy scalar; fine. No lambda.

Now, let's consider if `np.fft.rfft` of a length n with `_G_L` computed; if a size differs, `rfft` pads/truncates? It uses input length n if n not >= a.size? Actually `rfft(a, n)` if n given, uses first n samples? If a length n and n parameter L larger, it zero-pads. If a length L? Our a length _G_N <= L. If a length not _G_N due bug, could misalign. Ensure all arrays size _G_N. `_set_best` checks. Good.

In `_conv_self`, `c = c[:2*_G_N-1]`. If `irfft` length L, correct.

Now, possible issue in `_corr_from_fa`: `b = b[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]` returns view with negative stride. Then if `b.min()<0`, `np.maximum` creates contiguous. If not, B may be non-contiguous but dot okay. fine.

Now, final code length maybe large but okay.

Let's produce final. Ensure final output only code block.
</think>

```python
"""
Piecewise-constant + full-sequence refinement for minimizing
2*n*max(convolve(a,a))/(sum(a)**2).

Algorithm:
1. Start from the provided parent sequence if it is a reasonable length,
   otherwise from uniform.
2. Run a randomized search over piecewise-constant (block) sequences,
   coordinate-optimize block heights, and also run a small derivative-free
   optimizer over block parameters.
3. Refine the best array with log-domain L-BFGS on a smooth log-sum-exp
   approximation of the max convolution.
4. Finish with mirror-descent / active-constraint style local moves and
   small mass transfers.
The best sequence found by fast FFT evaluation is returned, and the last
candidates are checked with the exact evaluator before returning.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    import scipy.optimize
except Exception:
    scipy = None

# ----------------------------- globals -----------------------------
_G_N = 0
_G_L = 0
_G_BEST_A = None
_G_BEST_P = None
_G_BEST_SCORE = np.inf
_G_PARENT_A = None
_G_DEADLINE = 0.0
_G_CURRENT_BETA = 1000.0
_G_RNG = None
_G_DE_K = 0


# ----------------------------- basic helpers -----------------------------

def _fft_len(n):
    m = 2 * int(n) - 1
    L = 1
    while L < m:
        L <<= 1
    return L


def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    a = a / s
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s2 = float(a.sum())
    if s2 <= 1e-12:
        return None
    a = a / s2
    return a


def _get_parent():
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        arr = np.ones(6962, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.ndim != 1:
        arr = arr.ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        arr = np.ones(6962, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return arr


def _finalize_globals(total_budget, seed):
    global _G_N, _G_L, _G_BEST_A, _G_BEST_P, _G_BEST_SCORE
    global _G_PARENT_A, _G_DEADLINE, _G_CURRENT_BETA, _G_RNG

    start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        total = max(30.0, float(total_budget))
    except Exception:
        total = 1800.0
    # Leave a safety margin.
    remaining = max(60.0, min(1770.0, total - 30.0))
    _G_DEADLINE = start + remaining

    try:
        seed_int = int(seed)
    except Exception:
        seed_int = 12345
    _G_RNG = np.random.default_rng(seed_int)
    _G_CURRENT_BETA = 1000.0

    parent = _get_parent()
    if 1000 <= parent.size <= 30000:
        n = int(parent.size)
        parent = parent[:n]
    else:
        n = 6962
        parent = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    parent = _normalize(parent)
    if parent is None or parent.size != n:
        parent = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    _G_N = n
    _G_L = _fft_len(n)
    _G_PARENT_A = parent.copy()

    sc = _fast_score(parent)
    if not math.isfinite(sc):
        sc = 2.0
    _G_BEST_SCORE = sc
    _G_BEST_A = parent.copy()

    p = np.log(np.maximum(_G_BEST_A, 1e-30))
    p -= float(np.mean(p))
    _G_BEST_P = p

    print(f"[init] n={_G_N} fast_score(parent)={_G_BEST_SCORE:.10f}")


def _set_best(a, sc):
    global _G_BEST_A, _G_BEST_P, _G_BEST_SCORE
    if not math.isfinite(sc):
        return
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size != _G_N:
        return
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return
    a = a / s
    if sc < _G_BEST_SCORE - 1e-12:
        _G_BEST_SCORE = float(sc)
        _G_BEST_A = a.copy()
        p = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-30))
        p -= float(np.mean(p))
        _G_BEST_P = p


# ----------------------------- FFT objective -----------------------------

def _conv_self(a):
    n = _G_N
    L = _G_L
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, n=L)
    c = c[:2 * n - 1]
    # Remove tiny negative roundoff.
    if c.size:
        mn = float(c.min())
        if mn < 0.0:
            c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def _corr_from_fa(fa, weights):
    """
    For a_i >= 0 and weights w_k >= 0 (length 2n-1), return B_i =
    sum_{j=0}^{n-1} a_j w_{i+j}.
    """
    n = _G_N
    L = _G_L
    w_rev = weights[::-1]
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w_rev, n=L)
    b = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw, n=L)
    b = b[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
    if b.size:
        mn = float(b.min())
        if mn < 0.0:
            b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    return b


def _fast_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size != _G_N or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return np.inf
    S = float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(S) or S <= 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    c = _conv_self(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    m = float(c.max())
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * _G_N * m / (S * S))


def _softmax_p(p):
    n = _G_N
    ones = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if p is None:
        return ones
    z = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if z.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        return ones
    z -= float(np.mean(z))
    mz = float(np.max(z))
    if not math.isfinite(mz):
        return ones
    z -= mz
    # Avoid severe underflow.
    z = np.maximum(z, -745.0)
    a = np.exp(z)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return ones
    a = a / s
    floor = 1e-25 / float(n)
    if a.size and float(a.min()) < floor:
        a = np.maximum(a, floor)
        s2 = float(a.sum())
        if s2 > 0:
            a /= s2
    return a


def _smooth_objective(p):
    global _G_BEST_A, _G_BEST_P, _G_BEST_SCORE

    n = _G_N
    L = _G_L
    beta = _G_CURRENT_BETA
    if not math.isfinite(beta) or beta <= 1e-8:
        beta = 1000.0

    a = _softmax_p(p)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, n=L)
    c = c[:2 * n - 1]
    if c.size:
        mn = float(c.min())
        if mn < 0.0:
            c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)

    maxc = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    if not math.isfinite(maxc) or maxc < 0.0:
        maxc = 0.0

    exact = float(2.0 * n * maxc)
    if math.isfinite(exact) and exact < _G_BEST_SCORE - 1e-12:
        _G_BEST_SCORE = exact
        _G_BEST_A = a.copy()
        p_store = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        if p_store.size == n:
            p_store -= float(np.mean(p_store))
        _G_BEST_P = p_store

    if maxc <= 1e-300:
        return 0.0, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    z = beta * (c - maxc)
    z = np.maximum(z, -745.0)
    expz = np.exp(z)
    s = float(expz.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        s = 1.0
    weights = expz / s
    log_s = math.log(s)
    smooth = maxc + log_s / beta
    val = float(2.0 * n * smooth)

    B = _corr_from_fa(fa, weights)
    B = np.maximum(B, 0.0)
    grad_a = 4.0 * n * B
    dm = float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
    grad_p = a * (grad_a - dm)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_p)):
        grad_p = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        val = exact

    return val, grad_p


def _lbfgs_callback(xk, fk, gk, pk, lk, nk):
    if time.monotonic() > _G_DEADLINE:
        return True
    return False


def _run_lbfgs(p0, beta, maxfun=300, maxiter=120):
    global _G_CURRENT_BETA
    if scipy is None:
        return
    if p0 is None:
        return
    _G_CURRENT_BETA = float(beta)
    p0 = np.asarray(p0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if p0.size != _G_N:
        p0 = np.zeros(_G_N, dtype=np.float64)
    p0 -= float(np.mean(p0))
    try:
        scipy.optimize.minimize(
            _smooth_objective,
            p0,
            jac=True,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            callback=_lbfgs_callback,
            options={
                "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                "maxfun": int(maxfun),
                "ftol": 1e-12,
                "gtol": 1e-5,
                "maxcor": 10,
                "maxls": 20,
                "disp": False,
            }
        )
    except Exception as e:
        print("L-BFGS aborted:", e)


def _estimate_objective_time():
    global _G_CURRENT_BETA
    _G_CURRENT_BETA = 10000.0
    p = np.zeros(_G_N, dtype=np.float64)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        _smooth_objective(p)
    except Exception:
        return 1e-3
    dt = time.monotonic() - t0
    if not math.isfinite(dt) or dt <= 0.0:
        return 1e-3
    return max(1e-4, dt)


def _run_smooth_phase(stop_time):
    if scipy is None:
        print("[smooth] scipy unavailable, skipping L-BFGS phase")
        return

    t_obj = _estimate_objective_time()
    t_obj_used = max(1e-4, t_obj)
    T_table = [1.0, 3.0, 10.0, 30.0, 100.0, 30.0, 10.0, 3.0, 1.0, 30.0, 10.0, 30.0]
    runs = 0
    print(f"[smooth] obj_time={t_obj_used:.5f}s")

    while time.monotonic() < stop_time and runs < 50:
        maxc = _G_BEST_SCORE / (2.0 * _G_N)
        if not math.isfinite(maxc) or maxc <= 1e-15:
            maxc = 1.0 / float(_G_N)
        T = T_table[runs % len(T_table)]
        beta = max(1000.0, T / maxc)

        if _G_BEST_P is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(_G_BEST_P)):
            p = np.asarray(_G_BEST_P, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        else:
            p = np.zeros(_G_N, dtype=np.float64)

        if runs > 0:
            sigma = 0.005 + 0.005 * (1 + (runs % 3))
            p += _G_RNG.normal(0.0, sigma, _G_N)
            p -= float(np.mean(p))

        maxfun = int(min(900, max(30, 22.0 / (t_obj_used * 1.5))))
        maxiter = int(min(220, max(12, maxfun * 0.25)))

        before = _G_BEST_SCORE
        _run_lbfgs(p, beta, maxfun=maxfun, maxiter=maxiter)
        after = _G_BEST_SCORE
        runs += 1
        print(f"[smooth] run={runs} T={T:g} beta={beta:.6g} score {before:.10f} -> {after:.10f}")


# ----------------------------- block search -----------------------------

def _random_lengths(K, alpha):
    n = _G_N
    K = max(1, int(K))
    w = _G_RNG.dirichlet(alpha * np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64))
    lengths = np.maximum(1, np.floor(w * float(n)).astype(np.int64))
    rem = n - int(lengths.sum())

    if rem > 0:
        frac = w * float(n) - lengths.astype(np.float64)
        while rem > 0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(frac))
            frac[idx] -= 1e100
            lengths[idx] += 1
            rem -= 1
    elif rem < 0:
        while rem < 0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(lengths))
            if lengths[idx] <= 1:
                break
            lengths[idx] -= 1
            rem += 1
            if rem < 0 and np.max(lengths) <= 1:
                break

    # Final safety adjustment.
    rem = n - int(lengths.sum())
    while rem > 0:
        lengths[0] += 1
        rem -= 1
    while rem < 0:
        idx = int(np.argmax(lengths))
        if lengths[idx] <= 1:
            break
        lengths[idx] -= 1
        rem += 1

    return lengths.tolist()


def _random_heights(K, mode):
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, max(1, int(K)))
    amp = float(_G_RNG.uniform(0.2, 2.5))
    h = np.ones(max(1, int(K)), dtype=np.float64)

    if mode == 0:
        sig = float(_G_RNG.choice([0.2, 0.5, 1.0]))
        h = np.exp(_G_RNG.normal(0.0, sig, h.size))
    elif mode == 1:
        h = 1.0 + amp * (2.0 * t - 1.0)
    elif mode == 2:
        h = 1.0 + amp * t
    elif mode == 3:
        h = 1.0 + amp * (1.0 - t)
    elif mode == 4:
        h = 1.0 + amp * 4.0 * (t - 0.5) ** 2
    elif mode == 5:
        f = float(_G_RNG.uniform(1.0, 4.0))
        ph = float(_G_RNG.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        h = 1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * f * t + ph)
    elif mode == 6:
        f = float(_G_RNG.uniform(1.0, 4.0))
        ph = float(_G_RNG.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        h = 1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * f * t + ph)
    else:
        h = 1.0 + amp * _G_RNG.random(h.size)

    h = np.clip(h, 1e-4, None)
    s = float(h.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        h = np.ones_like(h)
        s = 1.0
    h = h / s
    return h


def _optimize_block_profile(lengths, heights, max_sweeps):
    lengths = np.asarray(lengths, dtype=np.int64)
    heights = np.asarray(heights, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    K = int(lengths.size)
    if K <= 0:
        return np.inf, None, None, None
    if lengths.size != K or int(lengths.sum()) != _G_N:
        return np.inf, None, None, None

    hs = float(heights.sum())
    if hs <= 1e-12:
        heights = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64) / float(K)
    else:
        heights = heights / hs

    a = np.repeat(heights, lengths)
    best_score = _fast_score(a)
    if not math.isfinite(best_score):
        return np.inf, a, heights, lengths

    starts = np.concatenate(([0], np.cumsum(lengths)[:-1]))
    ends = np.cumsum(lengths)
    factors = np.exp(np.linspace(-3.2, 3.2, 21))

    for _ in range(max(1, int(max_sweeps))):
        if time.monotonic() > _G_DEADLINE:
            break
        order = _G_RNG.permutation(K)
        improved = False

        for b in order:
            b = int(b)
            start = int(starts[b])
            stop = int(ends[b])
            orig = float(heights[b])

            if orig > 1e-12:
                base = orig
            else:
                pos = heights[heights > 1e-12]
                if pos.size:
                    base = float(np.min(pos))
                else:
                    base = 1.0 / float(_G_N)
            if base <= 1e-15:
                base = 1.0 / float(_G_N)

            local_best = best_score
            local_x = orig

            for f in factors:
                x = base * float(f)
                if x <= 1e-15:
                    x = 1e-15
                a[start:stop] = x
                sc = _fast_score(a)
                if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_best:
                    local_best = sc
                    local_x = x

            if local_x != orig:
                heights[b] = local_x
                a[start:stop] = local_x
                if local_best < best_score:
                    best_score = local_best
                    improved = True
            else:
                a[start:stop] = orig

        if not improved:
            break

        hs = float(heights.sum())
        if hs > 1e-12:
            heights = heights / hs
            a = np.repeat(heights, lengths)
            best_score = _fast_score(a)
            if not math.isfinite(best_score):
                break

    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s > 0.0:
        a = a / s
    else:
        a = np.ones(_G_N, dtype=np.float64) / float(_G_N)

    return best_score, a, heights, lengths


def _run_base_candidates():
    n = _G_N
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    profs = []

    profs.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    profs.append(x + 1e-6)
    profs.append((1.0 - x) + 1e-6)

    amps = [0.5, 1.0, 2.0]
    for amp in amps:
        profs.append(1.0 + amp * (2.0 * x - 1.0))
        profs.append(1.0 + amp * x)
        profs.append(1.0 + amp * (1.0 - x))
        profs.append(1.0 + amp * 4.0 * (x - 0.5) ** 2)
        profs.append(1.0 + amp / (x + 0.05))
        profs.append(1.0 + amp / (1.0 - x + 0.05))

    for f in [1.0, 2.0, 3.0]:
        for amp in [0.5, 1.0, 1.5]:
            profs.append(1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * f * x))
            profs.append(1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * f * x))

    for p in profs:
        a = _normalize(np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64))
        if a is None or a.size != n:
            continue
        sc = _fast_score(a)
        _set_best(a, sc)


def _run_block_search(gen_stop, opt_stop):
    candidates = []
    maxK = int(min(12, max(3, _G_N // 500)))

    while time.monotonic() < gen_stop and len(candidates) < 250:
        K = int(_G_RNG.integers(2, maxK + 1))
        alpha = float(_G_RNG.choice([0.3, 0.7, 1.0, 2.0]))
        lengths = _random_lengths(K, alpha)
        mode = int(_G_RNG.integers(0, 8))
        heights = _random_heights(K, mode)
        a = np.repeat(heights, lengths)
        sc = _fast_score(a)
        if math.isfinite(sc):
            _set_best(a, sc)
            candidates.append((float(sc), len(candidates), lengths, heights))

    if not candidates:
        return

    candidates.sort()
    top = candidates[:12]

    for sc, idx, lengths, heights in top:
        if time.monotonic() > opt_stop:
            break
        K = len(lengths)
        ms = 6 if K <= 5 else 4 if K <= 8 else 3
        sc2, a, h2, l2 = _optimize_block_profile(lengths, heights, ms)
        if math.isfinite(sc2) and a is not None:
            _set_best(a, sc2)
            print(f"[blocks] optimized K={K} score {sc:.8f} -> {sc2:.8f}")


# ----------------------------- low-dimensional DE over blocks -----------------------------

def _arrays_from_de_x(x, K):
    n = _G_N
    K = int(K)
    if K <= 0 or K > n:
        return None, None, None, np.inf

    lx = np.asarray(x[:K], dtype=np.float64)
    hl = np.asarray(x[K:2 * K], dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(lx)) or not np.all(np.isfinite(hl)):
        return None, None, None, np.inf

    ml = float(np.max(lx))
    el = np.exp(lx - ml)
    w = el / float(el.sum())
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(w)) or float(w.sum()) <= 0.0:
        return None, None, None, np.inf

    lengths = np.maximum(1, np.floor(w * float(n)).astype(np.int64))
    rem = n - int(lengths.sum())
    if rem > 0:
        fr = w * float(n) - lengths.astype(np.float64)
        while rem > 0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(fr))
            fr[idx] -= 1e100
            lengths[idx] += 1
            rem -= 1
    elif rem < 0:
        while rem < 0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(lengths))
            if lengths[idx] <= 1:
                break
            lengths[idx] -= 1
            rem += 1

    rem = n - int(lengths.sum())
    while rem > 0:
        lengths[0] += 1
        rem -= 1
    while rem < 0:
        idx = int(np.argmax(lengths))
        if lengths[idx] <= 1:
            break
        lengths[idx] -= 1
        rem += 1

    if int(lengths.sum()) != n:
        return None, None, None, np.inf

    mh = float(np.max(hl))
    eh = np.exp(hl - mh)
    hs = float(eh.sum())
    if hs <= 1e-12:
        h = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64) / float(K)
    else:
        h = eh / hs

    a = np.repeat(h, lengths)
    sc = _fast_score(a)
    return a, h, lengths, sc


def _de_block_objective(x):
    K = _G_DE_K
    if K <= 0:
        return 1e100
    _, _, _, sc = _arrays_from_de_x(x, K)
    if not math.isfinite(sc):
        return 1e100
    return float(sc)


def _run_de_block(stop_time):
    if scipy is None:
        return

    for K in (3, 4):
        if time.monotonic() > stop_time:
            break
        _G_DE_K = K
        bounds = [(-4.0, 4.0)] * (2 * K)
        maxiter = 14 if K <= 3 else 10
        try:
            res = scipy.optimize.differential_evolution(
                _de_block_objective,
                bounds,
                maxiter=maxiter,
                popsize=5,
                tol=0.1,
                seed=7,
                polish=False,
            )
        except Exception as e:
            print("DE block search aborted:", e)
            continue

        try:
            best_x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            a, h, l, sc = _arrays_from_de_x(best_x, K)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and a is not None:
                _set_best(a, sc)
                print(f"[de-block] K={K} score={sc:.10f}")
        except Exception:
            pass


# ----------------------------- mirror / transfer local phase -----------------------------

def _run_mirror_phase(stop_time):
    if _G_BEST_A is None:
        a = np.ones(_G_N, dtype=np.float64) / float(_G_N)
    else:
        a = np.asarray(_G_BEST_A, dtype=np.float64).copy()

    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-18)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        a = np.ones(_G_N, dtype=np.float64) / float(_G_N)
        s = 1.0
    a = a / s
    current = _fast_score(a)
    if not math.isfinite(current):
        a = np.ones(_G_N, dtype=np.float64) / float(_G_N)
        current = 2.0
    _set_best(a, current)

    n = _G_N
    modes = [-1.0, 0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.25]
    cycle = 0
    no_improve = 0
    etas = (0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.25)

    while time.monotonic() < stop_time:
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=_G_L)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, n=_G_L)[:2 * n - 1]
        if c.size:
            if float(c.min()) < 0.0:
                c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
            M = float(c.max())
        else:
            c = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
            M = 0.0

        if M <= 1e-300:
            break

        mode = modes[cycle % len(modes)]
        if mode < 0:
            tol = max(1e-12, 1e-7 * M)
            mask = (c >= (M - tol))
            if not np.any(mask):
                mask[np.argmax(c)] = True
            w = mask.astype(np.float64)
            ws = float(w.sum())
            if ws <= 0:
                w = np.zeros_like(c)
                w[np.argmax(c)] = 1.0
            else:
                w = w / ws
        else:
            temp = max(1e-300, mode * M)
            z = (c - M) / temp
            z = np.maximum(z, -745.0)
            e = np.exp(z)
            s = float(e.sum())
            if s <= 1e-300 or not math.isfinite(s):
                w = np.zeros_like(c)
                w[np.argmax(c)] = 1.0
            else:
                w = e / s

        B = _corr_from_fa(fa, w)
        B = np.maximum(B, 0.0)
        grad = 4.0 * n * B
        raw = grad - float(np.mean(grad))
        rmax = float(np.max(np.abs(raw)))

        improved = False
        if math.isfinite(rmax) and rmax > 1e-14:
            theta = raw / rmax
            for eta in etas:
                shift = -float(eta) * theta
                shift -= float(np.max(shift))
                new = a * np.exp(shift)
                ns = float(new.sum())
                if ns <= 1e-12 or not math.isfinite(ns):
                    continue
                new = new / ns
                sc = _fast_score(new)
                if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < current - 1e-12:
                    a = new
                    current = sc
                    improved = True
                    if sc < _G_BEST_SCORE - 1e-12:
                        _set_best(a, current)
                        print(f"[mirror] improved to {sc:.10f}")
                    break

        if not improved:
            order = np.argsort(raw)
            top = 2
            sources = order[-top:]
            sinks = order[:top]
            for i in sources:
                if improved:
                    break
                i = int(i)
                for j in sinks:
                    if improved:
                        break
                    j = int(j)
                    if i == j:
                        continue
                    ai = float(a[i])
                    if ai <= 1e-15:
                        continue
                    for frac in (0.02, 0.08):
                        q = ai * float(frac)
                        if q <= 1e-15:
                            continue
                        new = a.copy()
                        new[i] = ai - q
                        new[j] = float(new[j]) + q
                        ns = float(new.sum())
                        if ns <= 1e-12 or not math.isfinite(ns):
                            continue
                        new = new / ns
                        sc = _fast_score(new)
                        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < current - 1e-12:
                            a = new
                            current = sc
                            improved = True
                            if sc < _G_BEST_SCORE - 1e-12:
                                _set_best(a, current)
                                print(f"[transfer] improved to {sc:.10f}")
                            break

        if not improved:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 20:
                sigma = 0.01 + 0.005 * min(5, no_improve // 10)
                noisy = a * np.exp(_G_RNG.normal(0.0, sigma, n))
                noisy = np.maximum(noisy, 1e-18)
                ns = float(noisy.sum())
                if ns > 0.0 and math.isfinite(ns):
                    noisy = noisy / ns
                    sc = _fast_score(noisy)
                    if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < _G_BEST_SCORE + 0.02:
                        a = noisy
                        current = sc
                no_improve = 0

        cycle += 1

    _set_best(a, current)


# ----------------------------- exact final check -----------------------------

def _exact_score(seq):
    try:
        v = evaluate_sequence(seq)
    except Exception:
        return np.inf
    try:
        v = float(v)
    except Exception:
        return np.inf
    if not np.isfinite(v):
        return np.inf
    return v


def _main(seed, budget_s):
    _finalize_globals(budget_s, seed)

    # Broad but cheap structured/random starts.
    _run_base_candidates()

    # Block search and coordinate optimization.
    gen_stop = max(_G_DEADLINE - 1500.0, time.monotonic() + 5.0)
    opt_stop = max(_G_DEADLINE - 1200.0, gen_stop + 5.0)
    if time.monotonic() < opt_stop:
        _run_block_search(gen_stop, opt_stop)

    # Small low-dimensional derivative-free block search.
    de_stop = max(_G_DEADLINE - 1000.0, opt_stop + 5.0)
    de_stop = min(de_stop, opt_stop + 180.0)
    if time.monotonic() < de_stop:
        _run_de_block(de_stop)

    print(f"[after blocks] fast_score={_G_BEST_SCORE:.10f}")

    # Full-array smooth refinement.
    smooth_stop = _G_DEADLINE - 330.0
    if time.monotonic() < smooth_stop:
        _run_smooth_phase(smooth_stop)

    print(f"[after smooth] fast_score={_G_BEST_SCORE:.10f}")

    # Local mirror / transfer polish.
    mirror_stop = _G_DEADLINE - 60.0
    if time.monotonic() < mirror_stop:
        _run_mirror_phase(mirror_stop)

    print(f"[final fast] score={_G_BEST_SCORE:.10f}")

    # Exact validation of best candidates.
    final_arrays = []
    if _G_BEST_A is not None:
        final_arrays.append(_G_BEST_A)
    if _G_PARENT_A is not None:
        final_arrays.append(_G_PARENT_A)
    final_arrays.append(np.ones(_G_N, dtype=np.float64))

    best_seq = None
    best_val = np.inf

    for arr in final_arrays:
        a = _normalize(arr)
        if a is None or a.size != _G_N:
            continue
        seq = [float(v) for v in a]
        val = _exact_score(seq)
        if math.isfinite(val) and val < best_val:
            best_val = val
            best_seq = seq

    if best_seq is None or best_val >= np.inf:
        fallback = np.ones(_G_N, dtype=np.float64) / float(_G_N)
        best_seq = [float(v) for v in fallback]
        best_val = _exact_score(best_seq)

    if not math.isfinite(best_val):
        fallback = np.ones(6962, dtype=np.float64) / 6962.0
        best_seq = [float(v) for v in fallback]
        best_val = _exact_score(best_seq)

    print(f"final exact score = {best_val:.12f}")
    return best_seq


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    return _main(seed, budget_s)
```
